Abstract: In recent years, electronic (E) commerce services have rapidly increased in the daily lives of people, which helpsthem to purchase products online. However, retail platforms have struggled to understand customer behavior and make it difficult to predict their future purchases. To overcome these challenges, this study proposes a hybrid Retail Deep NeuralNetwork (Ret-DNN) with an Extreme Gradient Boosting(XGBoost) model for capturing temporal features and tabular dynamics of retail data. First, data were sourced from a UnitedKingdom (UK)-based online retailer that contains transactions with almost 500,000 records. Then, the collected data were pre-processed using a series of techniques, such as data cleaning, outlier handling, temporal feature extraction, feature encoding, and z-score normalization, to ensure that the data were ready for model training and testing. Subsequently, the preprocessed data were fed into the Ret-DNN model, which acts as a feature extractor to understand the complete context of customer transactions. Further, the extracted data were fed as input into the XGBoost model, which predicted the final output as the purchase probability of customers. Finally, the proposed Ret-DNN XGBoost model achieved better results by attaining aMean Absolute Error (MAE) 0.2193 when compared to the existing Ret-DNN model.
  Keywords: Customer behavior forecasting, extreme gradientboosting, electronic commerce, predictive analytic, retail deepneural networks.

</details>


### [76] [SoftMoE: Soft Differentiable Routing for Mixture-of-Experts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17952)
*Mikołaj Zasada,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Kurdziel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftMoE 使用可微的软 top-k 路由（LapSum 松弛）替代离散 top-k 路由，允许梯度优化专家分配，并在全局预算约束下学习每层的平均专家激活数，实现更高效的计算利用，在语言建模和下游任务上性能相当或更优，且激活专家数显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏 MoE 使用离散 top-k 路由不可微，导致每输入固定激活专家数，计算利用不高效。

Method: 用截断的软 top-k LapSum 松弛替代离散路由，参数化每层平均激活专家数并施加全局预算约束，使模型可端到端学习专家分配。

Result: 性能与稀疏 MoE 相当或更优，激活专家数显著减少；学到非均匀分配，深层激活更多专家。

Conclusion: SoftMoE 通过可微分路由和可学习预算分配，在保持自回归建模兼容性的同时，提升了 MoE 的计算效率。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable scaling LLM parameters under a fixed inference budget by activating only a small subset of experts via top-$k$ routing. While this preserves causality and suits autoregressive language models, the discrete top-$k$ operator is not differentiable, forcing a fixed number of active experts per input and resulting in inefficient use of computation. We propose SoftMoE, which replaces discrete routing with a truncated soft top-$k$ LapSum relaxation, allowing gradient-based optimization of expert routing. We further parameterize the mean number of active experts per layer and impose a global budget constraint, enabling the model to learn how to allocate expert capacity across layers. SoftMoE remains fully compatible with autoregressive modeling and achieves performance comparable to or better than sparse MoE on language modeling and downstream tasks, while activating significantly fewer experts. Notably, the learned allocation is highly non-uniform, with later layers activating more experts. The source code is publicly available$^\dagger$.

</details>


### [77] [Multiple cyclicity and Wavelet Decomposition with Channel Correlation for Long-term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17996)
*Bin Wang,Heming Yang,Jinfang Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: McWC是一种长期时间序列预测模型，通过分别建模周期性、趋势和通道间相关性，并采用多层周期性构建、MLP提取通道相关性、多级小波分解融合高低频信息，在六个真实数据集上取得了最先进性能，同时具备高计算效率和历史信息提取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测工作忽视了真实世界中通道间相关性的影响，导致预测次优；同时，现有模型依赖复杂设计捕获多样信息，计算效率低下。

Method: McWC模型包含四个核心模块：(1)多层周期性构建模块，从数据中解耦周期信息；(2)多层感知机（MLP），提取通道间相关性；(3)多级小波分解模块，建模并融合多层高低频信息；(4)聚合各组件结果获得输出。同时在频域计算损失函数以解耦通道内自相关。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上，McWC实现了最先进的预测性能，同时展现出优秀的计算效率和历史信息提取能力。

Conclusion: 通过分别建模周期性、趋势和通道间相关性，McWC有效解决了现有模型忽视通道间相关性及计算效率低下的问题，验证了分离建模各时间序列成分的有效性。

Abstract: Cyclicity and trend are important components of time series data and many studies based on cyclicity and trend have achieved good results in long-term time series forecasting. However, we believe that current work neglects the influence of real-world inter-channel correlations in time series data which leads to suboptimal predictions. Furthermore, these models rely on complex designs to capture diverse information so that resulting in low computational efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose McWC, a long-term time series forecasting model that separately models the cyclicity, trend, and inter-channel correlations. Specifically, McWC first decouples cyclical information from data using a multi-layer cyclicity construction module. Then, it extracts inter-channel correlations using multi-layer perceptron. Next, it models and fuses the multi-layer high-frequency and low-frequency information from data using a multi-level wavelet decomposition module. Finally, it aggregates the results of different components to obtain the output. Simultaneously, we decouple intra-channel autocorrelations by calculating a loss function in the frequency domain. Experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that McWC achieves state-of-the-art performance, exhibiting excellent computational efficiency and historical information extraction capabilities.

</details>


### [78] [Half a Link can Be Enough to Predict a Whole Link: Understanding Generalization in Knowledge Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18001)
*Cosimo Gregucci,Obaidah Theeb,Daniel Hernandez,Antonio Vergari,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了知识图谱基础模型在零样本泛化中的非均匀表现，发现模型依赖"半链接"（即部分已见的(h,r)或(r,t)）进行预测，并据此提出了四类场景的分类法，可用作KGFM鲁棒泛化的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGFM虽能零样本泛化，但其泛化机制尚不明确，尤其在不同类型链接上的表现差异未被系统研究。

Method: 定义"半链接"概念，将测试三元组按(h,r)和(r,t)是否在推理图中出现划分为四种场景，对SOTA KGFM进行分层分析。

Result: SOTA KGFM在已见半链接场景下表现良好，而未见过半链接带来显著挑战；模型性能在不同场景间非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 提出的细粒度分类法可作为KGFM鲁棒泛化的诊断协议，为未来模型改进指明方向。

Abstract: Knowledge graph (KG) foundation models (KGFMs) are zero-shot generalizers: trained once, they can predict links on unseen graphs without retraining. However, understanding when and how they can robustly generalize across KGs is still an open question. In this paper, we shed some light on their generalization mechanisms highlighting how their performance on unseen KGs is not uniform when it comes to partially seen links, which we call half-links. In fact, we show that to predict a test triple $(h,r,t)$ it might suffice in practice to have observed the half-link $(h,r)$ or $(r,t)$ in the inference graph. This yields a taxonomy of four scenarios when combinations of these half-links are observed or not. In a rigorous stratified analysis over these scenarios, we reveal that SoTA KGFMs use seen half links for predictions, while unseen half-links pose different challenges. As such, our finer-grained taxonomy can be a diagnostic protocol for robust KGFM generalization and highlights where novel KGFMs can improve.

</details>


### [79] [C2FL: Clustered Continual Federated Learning under Spatial and Temporal Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18003)
*Davide Domini,Gianluca Aguzzi,Lorenzo Pellegrini,Mirko Viroli,Lukas Esterle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出C2FL，一种完全分布式联邦学习方法，通过空间聚类自组织学习群组，并利用经验回放与驻留时间感知的自适应平均来应对时空漂移，在保护隐私的同时实现鲁棒的集体自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体自适应系统(CAS)中节点感知数据具有隐私敏感性、空间异质性和时间漂移等特征，传统集中式学习和标准联邦学习策略难以有效应对这些挑战。

Method: C2FL方法：1) 节点通过空间聚类自组织形成学习群组；2) 结合经验回放与驻留时间感知的自适应平均步骤，节点在同一区域停留越久越融入区域共识，同时保留先前知识以应对分布演化。

Result: 在系统重现空间和时间偏移的合成实验中，标准联邦策略性能严重下降，而C2FL恢复了鲁棒的集体自适应能力。

Conclusion: C2FL能有效解决隐私约束下CAS中空间异质性与时间漂移耦合带来的学习退化问题，实现分布式场景下的鲁棒集体学习。

Abstract: Collective Adaptive Systems (CAS) increasingly rely on machine learning to let each node learn from locally sensed data, aligning its behavior with the surrounding environment. Scaling this intelligence, however, raises fundamental challenges: sensed data is often privacy-sensitive, preventing centralized collection; nodes are mobile, traversing regions where nearby nodes perceive similar phenomena while distant ones observe radically different conditions, creating natural spatial clusters; and these distributions evolve over time due to mobility, introducing temporal drift that makes local models progressively stale. These dynamics arise across domains - vehicular sensing, drone-based monitoring, smartphone crowdsensing - yet the interplay of privacy, spatial heterogeneity, and temporal drift severely undermines conventional learning strategies. Therefore, we propose C2FL, a fully distributed Federated Learning (FL) approach where nodes self-organize into learning groups through spatial clustering, reflecting the geographic structure of the environment. To counteract temporal drift, each node combines experience replay with a dwell-time-aware adaptive averaging step, progressively incorporating the regional consensus as it remains longer within the same area, while preserving previously acquired knowledge under evolving distributions. We evaluate our approach on synthetic experiments that systematically reproduce spatial and temporal shifts, showing that standard federated strategies degrade significantly under these conditions and that our method restores robust collective adaptation.

</details>


### [80] [Recursive Scaling in Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18022)
*Alba Carballo-Castro,Julianna Piskorz,Paulius Rauba,Mihaela van der Schaar,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了递归掩码扩散模型（R-MDMs），通过在去噪步骤中重复使用相同的Transformer进行递归计算，为扩散模型增加了“递归深度”这一新的扩展维度。实验表明，R-MDMs在Sudoku和Countdown等结构化生成任务中实现了显著的参数效率提升：L次递归迭代可匹配约L倍参数的非递归基线模型性能，且递归能部分替代去噪步骤，减少推理时的前向传播次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的掩码扩散模型扩展方式仅依赖于增加参数量或去噪步数，缺乏其他有效的扩展维度。作者希望探索递归深度作为第三种扩展轴的可能性，在不增加参数量的前提下提升模型性能。

Method: 在每个扩散去噪步骤中，重复应用同一个去噪Transformer进行递归计算（即参数复用），通过递归迭代实现模型有效深度的增加，而参数量保持不变。

Result: 在Sudoku和Countdown等结构化生成任务中，拥有L次递归迭代的R-MDMs性能接近参数量约L倍的非递归基线模型；递归还能部分替代额外的去噪步骤，用更少的前向传播达到相同的生成质量。

Conclusion: 递归深度是掩码扩散模型的一种实用扩展机制，能够同时提升参数效率和测试时计算资源的分配效率。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for sequence generation. Scaling MDMs is conventionally achieved by increasing the parameter count or the number of denoising steps. We introduce Recursive Masked Diffusion Models (R-MDMs), which add recursive depth as a third scaling axis by repeatedly applying the same denoising transformer within each diffusion step. Recursion enables iterative refinement of the output through parameter reuse, increasing effective model depth without increasing parameter count. Across structured generation tasks, including Sudoku and Countdown, we show that R-MDMs achieve substantially improved parameter efficiency: a model with $L$ recursive iterations often matches the performance of non-recursive baselines with roughly $L\times$ more parameters. Moreover, recursive refinement can partially substitute for additional denoising steps, allowing recursive models to reach the same generation quality with fewer forward passes at inference time. These results suggest that recursive depth is a practically useful scaling mechanism for MDMs, improving both parameter efficiency and the allocation of test-time compute.

</details>


### [81] [LoopCoder-v2: Only Loop Once for Efficient Test-Time Computation Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18023)
*Jian Yang,Shawn Guo,Wei Zhang,Tianyu Zheng,Yaxin Du,Haau-Sing Li,Jiajun Wu,Yue Song,Yan Xing,Qingsong Cai,Zelong Huang,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Xianglong Liu,Wayne Xin Zhao,Mingjie Tang,Weifeng Lv,Ming Zhou,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并行循环Transformer（PLT），通过跨循环位置偏移（CLP）和共享KV门控滑动窗口注意力解决循环Transformer的延迟和KV缓存问题，并系统研究了PLT循环次数的选择。实验发现2循环变体在代码生成、推理、软件工程等任务上显著优于无循环基线（SWE-bench从43.0提升至64.4），而3个及以上循环反而性能下降，揭示了增益-成本权衡：第2循环提供主要精炼增益，后续循环增益递减且CLP引入的位置偏移成本固定，导致净收益为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重复共享块扩展潜在计算能力，但顺序循环导致延迟和KV缓存内存随循环次数线性增长。为缓解此成本，作者提出PLT架构。在此基础上，需要系统研究如何选择最优循环次数，理解不同循环次数带来的增益与成本之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出并行循环Transformer（PLT），核心设计包括跨循环位置偏移（CLP）和共享KV门控滑动窗口注意力。通过训练LoopCoder-v2系列模型（7B参数，不同循环次数），从零开始在18T token上预训练，然后进行匹配的指令微调。从增益-成本视角分析循环次数选择：额外循环可以精炼表示，但CLP在每个循环边界引入位置偏移。

Result: 2循环变体在代码生成、代码推理、智能体软件工程和工具使用基准上全面优于无循环基线，SWE-bench Verified从43.0提升至64.4，Multi-SWE从14.0提升至31.0。3个及以上循环变体性能下降，呈现强非单调的循环次数效应。诊断发现第2循环提供主要生产性精炼，后续循环更新递减、振荡且表示多样性降低。CLP引起的偏移成本大致固定，而精炼增益收缩，导致偏移成本逐渐主导。

Conclusion: PLT的增益-成本权衡解释了在2循环处饱和的现象：第2循环提供主要精炼增益，后续循环的边际收益递减而CLP位置偏移成本固定，净收益变负。这为循环次数选择提供了诊断依据，建议PLT架构中最优循环次数为2。

Abstract: Looped Transformers scale latent computation by repeatedly applying shared blocks, but sequential looping increases latency and KV-cache memory with the loop count. Parallel loop Transformers (PLT) alleviate this cost through cross-loop position offsets (CLP) and shared-KV gated sliding-window attention, making loop count a practical design choice. We therefore study PLT loop-count selection through a gain--cost view: an extra loop may refine representations, but CLP also introduces a positional mismatch at each loop boundary. We instantiate this study by training LoopCoder-v2, a family of 7B PLT coders with different loop counts, from scratch on 18T tokens, followed by matched instruction tuning and evaluation. Empirically, the two-loop variant delivers broad gains over the non-looped baseline across code generation, code reasoning, agentic software engineering, and tool-use benchmarks, improving SWE-bench Verified from 43.0 to 64.4 points and Multi-SWE from 14.0 to 31.0 points. In contrast, variants with three or more loops regress, revealing a strongly non-monotonic loop-count effect. Our diagnostics show that loop 2 provides the main productive refinement, while later loops yield diminishing, oscillatory updates and reduced representational diversity. Because the CLP-induced mismatch remains roughly fixed as refinement gains shrink, the offset cost increasingly dominates. This gain--cost trade-off explains PLT's saturation at two loops and provides diagnostics for loop-count selection.

</details>


### [82] [Catastrophic Forgetting is Low-Rank: A Function-Space Theory for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18024)
*Ido Nitzan Hidekel,Dan Raviv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在神经切线核（NTK）框架下建立了持续学习中灾难性遗忘的函数空间解释：新任务训练通过跨任务核诱导旧任务预测漂移，推导出遗忘向量的闭式解，并发现遗忘集中在少数NTK本征模态中，据此提出了一种谱正则化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续适应中的灾难性遗忘研究多从参数漂移、重放或蒸馏角度出发，未能识别输出空间中哪些方向是脆弱的，缺乏函数空间层面的理解。

Method: 在NTK机制下给出遗忘向量的闭式预测器：新任务训练通过跨任务核导致旧任务预测漂移；在冻结骨干、线性头的PEFT-CL中预测器精确成立，在非线性适配器/全微调中为局部NTK近似；通过该表达式揭示遗忘集中在少数旧任务NTK本征模态中，并提出Kronecker缩放规则用于刻画脆弱秩。

Result: 推导出遗忘向量的闭式解；发现遗忘集中在少量NTK本征模态；建立了与先验NTK重叠理论的关系；解释了参数空间正则化器为何无法捕捉输出空间干扰；提出了一种针对性的谱正则化器。

Conclusion: 该函数空间视角为持续学习中的灾难性遗忘提供了新的理解，揭示了遗忘的本质结构，并启发了更有效的正则化策略。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting in continual adaptation is usually studied through parameter drift, replay, or distillation, but these views do not identify which output-space directions are vulnerable. We give a function-space account in the NTK regime: new-task training induces old-task prediction drift through the cross-task kernel, yielding a closed-form predictor for the forgetting vector before any new-task gradient step. In frozen-backbone linear-head PEFT-CL, where the model is linear in the trainable parameters, the predictor is exact up to numerical precision; for nonlinear adapters/full fine-tuning, it is a local NTK approximation. The same expression reveals that forgetting concentrates in a small number of old-task NTK eigenmodes and under frozen linear heads gives a Kronecker scaling rule for the vulnerable rank. These results clarify the relation to prior NTK-overlap theory, explain why parameter-space regularizers can miss output-space interference, and motivate a targeted spectral regularizer.

</details>


### [83] [ConTex: Reformulating Counterfactual Generation For Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18049)
*Jan Voets,Hasan Tercan,Tobias Meisen,Sebastian Baum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ConTex 框架，将时间序列预测中的反事实解释生成问题重新定义为学习全局一致的干预策略，通过分解式架构（时序上下文编码器+条件编码器+两个输出头）实现单次前向传播即可生成稀疏、可解释的反事实，相比实例级优化方法计算成本降低12-36倍，支持实时推理（约0.007秒），并在多个预测架构和基准数据集上达到最优有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的时序预测模型虽预测准确，但无法提供 actionable insights（可操作的洞见），即如何修改当前条件以将预测结果转向期望的未来场景。当前的反事实解释方法依赖实例级优化，存在跨实例不一致、计算成本高、难以实时应用等局限。

Method: ConTex（Counterfactual Time Series Explanations）：一个模型无关的分解式架构，包含时序上下文编码器（temporal context encoder）和条件编码器（conditional encoder），后接两个输出头分别捕获干预的时间相关性（temporal relevance）和修改强度（modification strength），通过单个共享函数学习全局一致的干预策略，在单次前向传播中生成反事实。

Result: 在多种预测架构和基准数据集上，ConTex达到state-of-the-art的有效性（validity），生成稀疏反事实以最小化必要干预次数；计算成本较实例级生成降低至少12-36倍；支持实时推理，推理时间约0.007秒。

Conclusion: ConTex通过全局一致的分解式架构，有效克服了实例级反事实生成方法的不稳定性和不一致性，实现了高效、实时、可解释的时序预测反事实解释，为决策支持提供了实用的干预指导。

Abstract: Decision-making with deep learning-based time series forecasting requires not only accurate predictions but also actionable insights. However, current architectures do not inherently provide such information. Specifically, guidance is needed on how current conditions must be modified to shift from a predicted outcome to a desired future scenario. Counterfactual explanations provide a natural framework for this task, as they represent minimal input changes that alter the model's prediction, indicating when and how intervention is required. Existing approaches rely on instance-wise optimization, leading to inconsistency across instances, high computational costs, and limited applicability in real-time settings.
  To address these limitations, we reformulate counterfactual generation for time series forecasting as the problem of learning a globally consistent intervention strategy, allowing counterfactuals to be generated through a single shared function. We propose Counterfactual Time Series Explanations (ConTex), a model-agnostic, decomposed architecture comprising a temporal context encoder and a conditional encoder, followed by two heads that capture interventions in terms of temporal relevance and modification strength. This structure overcomes the instability and inconsistency of instance-based approaches by producing targeted, interpretable interventions across time and feature dimensions in a single forward pass, making it suitable for real-time applications.
  Across multiple forecasting architectures and benchmark datasets, ConTex achieves state-of-the-art validity while generating sparse counterfactuals that minimize the number of necessary interventions. Additionally, our approach reduces computational cost by at least 12-36x compared to instance-wise generation and supports real-time inference at approximately 0.007 seconds.

</details>


### [84] [NoiseTilt: Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernels for Diffusion Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18066)
*Jisung Hwang,Yunhong Min,Jaihoon Kim,I-Chao Shen,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出噪声倾斜逆核（NTRK），一种通过将奖励梯度注入噪声项而非改变反向均值来引导扩散采样的方法，在保持预训练模型不变的同时实现单步单样本采样，在奖励对齐任务上超越现有基线且不损失质量，并在美学生成任务中以25 NFEs达到基线500 NFEs的奖励水平（20倍计算缩减）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励引导的扩散采样方法存在权衡：基于梯度的方法通过偏移反向均值引导生成，但会将中间状态推离训练分布导致质量下降；基于搜索的方法能保持质量但缺乏梯度信号。尚无方法能同时兼顾两者。

Method: NTRK保持反向均值固定，将奖励梯度偏置到噪声项中。核心是引入白化算子（whitening operator），使奖励梯度能够安全地作为噪声注入而不丢失引导信号，从而实现单步单样本采样。

Result: 在各种奖励对齐任务中，NTRK在保持样本质量不变的前提下超越了最新基线。在美学生成任务中，NTRK仅用25次NFE就达到了最佳基线500次NFE的奖励水平，计算量降低20倍。

Conclusion: NTRK通过固定反向均值并利用白化算子将奖励梯度安全注入噪声项，同时实现了高质量和梯度引导，打破了现有方法在这两者之间的权衡困境。

Abstract: We introduce the Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernel (NTRK), a reward-guided diffusion sampler that injects reward gradients through the noise term, leaving the pretrained reverse kernel unchanged and requiring only a single sample per step. Reward-guided sampling at inference time has greatly expanded the versatility of pretrained diffusion models. Yet existing methods face a trade-off. Gradient-based guidance shifts the reverse mean, steering generation but pushing intermediate states outside the region that the model was trained on and degrading quality. Search-based methods preserve quality but gain no gradient signal. No prior method achieves both. NTRK resolves this by keeping the reverse mean fixed and biasing the noise term toward high reward. We introduce a whitening operator, the central mechanism behind NTRK, that makes the reward gradient safe to inject as noise without losing its guiding signal. Across various reward alignment tasks, NTRK outperforms recent state-of-the-art baselines without losing sample quality. Remarkably, on aesthetic generation, NTRK surpasses the reward of the best baseline at 500 NFEs using only 25 NFEs, a 20$\times$ reduction in compute.

</details>


### [85] [Volterra Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18071)
*Yusen Jia,Bingyan Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Volterra生成模型，一种基于分数核路径依赖噪声的连续时间得分匹配框架，通过有限维马尔可夫提升处理非马尔可夫/非半鞅动力学，并解决协方差退化问题，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上展示了持久扰动对生成效果的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准得分扩散模型使用布朗运动扰动，虽具有可逆的逆向动力学，但噪声是马尔可夫（无记忆）的。作者希望引入具有记忆效应的路径依赖噪声（分数核），以提升生成模型的表达能力。

Method: 1）定义分数核路径依赖噪声的前向过程；2）通过高斯求积构造有限维马尔可夫提升，在平滑区域使用混合有限差指数近似；3）导出增广线性高斯前向过程；4）利用残差状态和解析辅助高斯得分保持数据维度学习；5）针对共享布朗因子和有符号权重导致的退化，提出稳定条件化和高斯桥重建采样器。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上，使用小规模马尔可夫提升的持久分数扰动改善了MNIST上的得分生成，并在自然图像上提供了有前景的扩展；桥采样器为大提升提供了稳定机制。

Conclusion: Volterra生成模型通过引入路径依赖噪声扩展了扩散模型框架，解决了非马尔可夫动力学的学习与采样挑战，实验表明该方法在图像生成任务上具有潜力。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models typically use Brownian perturbations, which provide tractable reverse-time dynamics but impose memoryless noising. We introduce Volterra generative models, a continuous-time score-based framework whose forward process injects path-dependent noise through fractional kernels. To handle the non-Markovian and non-semimartingale dynamics, we construct finite-dimensional Markovian lifts using Gaussian quadrature in both regimes and a hybrid finite-difference exponential approximation in the smooth regime. We prove squared error bounds, derive an augmented linear-Gaussian forward process, and show that the learning can remain data-dimensional by considering residual states and analytic auxiliary Gaussian scores. We also identify covariance and reverse-time degeneracies caused by shared Brownian factors and signed smooth-regime weights. The degeneracy motivates stabilized conditioning and, for stiff larger lifts, a Gaussian-bridge reconstruction sampler. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that persistent fractional perturbations with small Markovian lifts can improve score-based generation on MNIST and provide a promising extension to natural images, while the bridge sampler provides a stability mechanism for larger lifts.

</details>


### [86] [Edge Flow: A Tractable and Predictive Continuous-Time Model for Gradient Descent at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18080)
*Pierre Marion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Edge Flow，一个由三个耦合常微分方程组成的系统，用于在"边缘稳定性"（EoS）机制下对梯度下降动力学进行建模。该模型将动力学分解为中心、振荡方向和振荡幅度，通过自稳定反馈回路实现锐度稳定，仅需两次梯度计算和一次Hessian-向量乘积即可离散化，在追踪梯度下降动力学方面优于现有连续时间EoS模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度学习的边缘稳定性（EoS）机制中，损失Hessian的最大特征值接近稳定阈值2/η，经典的分析工具（如梯度流和下降引理）在此不再适用，因此需要寻找一种在EoS下有效的梯度下降连续时间模型。

Method: 提出Edge Flow，由三个耦合常微分方程组成，将动力学分解为中心（沿对称化损失的修正梯度流）、振荡方向（通过Rayleigh商动力学追踪Hessian的顶特征向量）和振荡幅度（根据锐度是否超过阈值2/η呈指数增长或衰减）。锐度稳定通过耦合动力学中的自稳定反馈回路实现。离散化仅需两次梯度评估和一次Hessian-向量乘积。

Result: 实验表明，Edge Flow在追踪梯度下降动力学方面至少与先前提出的连续时间EoS模型同样忠实，并且能够解析EoS初始阶段的锐度振荡，为该机制下的不稳定性理解和缓解提供了原则性框架。

Conclusion: Edge Flow提供了一个可处理、忠实且可预测的梯度下降在EoS机制下的动力学模型，通过自稳定反馈回路解释了锐度稳定现象，并为理解和缓解该机制下的不稳定性提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Gradient descent in deep learning may operate at the edge of stability (EoS), a regime in which the largest eigenvalue of the loss Hessian hovers near the stability threshold $2/η$, where $η$ is the learning rate. Classical analysis tools such as gradient flow and the descent lemma do not apply here, motivating the search for a continuous-time model valid at EoS. We propose Edge Flow, a system of three coupled ordinary differential equations that provides a tractable, faithful, and predictive model of gradient descent dynamics at EoS. Edge Flow decomposes the dynamics into a center, an oscillation direction, and an oscillation magnitude. The center follows a modified gradient flow on a symmetrized loss; the direction tracks a top eigenvector of the Hessian via Rayleigh quotient dynamics; and the magnitude grows or decays exponentially depending on whether the sharpness exceeds or falls below the threshold $2/η$. Crucially, sharpness stabilization emerges from the coupled dynamics via a self-stabilization feedback loop. Discretizing Edge Flow only requires two gradient evaluations and one Hessian--vector product at each iteration. We demonstrate empirically that Edge Flow tracks the dynamics of gradient descent at least as faithfully as previously proposed continuous-time EoS models, while in addition resolving the oscillation of the sharpness at the onset of EoS, and that it provides a principled framework for understanding and mitigating instabilities in this regime.

</details>


### [87] [From Reasoning Traces to Reusable Modules: Understanding Compositional Generalization in Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18089)
*Lingjing Kong,Xin Liu,Guangyi Chen,Martin Q. Ma,Xiangchen Song,Yuekai Sun,Mikhail Yurochkin,Taylor W. Killian,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Kun Zhang,Eric P. Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了SFT和RL在推理能力提升中扮演不对称互补角色的理论：SFT提供组合式轨迹中的原始模块材料，RL分解这些轨迹以识别潜在原子模块并实现组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何SFT+RL的后训练流程能有效将LLM转化为稳健推理器，揭示其成功的底层机制。

Method: 通过层次化潜在选择模型形式化组合泛化，将推理轨迹建模为离散潜在选择变量的级联；理论分析SFT与RL的非对称互补作用；设计受控实验验证理论。

Result: RL能从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块并重新组合解决新配置；训练复合轨迹比训练孤立原子模块泛化更强；识别出有效协议：SFT确保原子模块覆盖，RL聚焦SFT支持范围外的新组合。

Conclusion: SFT与RL的组合成功归因于组合泛化，两者通过层次化潜在选择模型实现非对称互补，为设计更高效的后训练流程提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Post-training pipelines that combine supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL) have emerged as the key recipe for transforming large language models (LLMs) into robust reasoners. We argue that this combined success is driven by compositional generalization, which we formalize through a hierarchical latent selection model. In this framework, reasoning traces are generated by a cascade of discrete latent selection variables corresponding to reusable atomic modules, including both skills (local operations) and routing mechanisms (how intermediate information is selected, reused, and composed). Within this model, we theoretically show that SFT and RL play asymmetric, complementary roles: SFT supplies the raw module materials in compositional traces, and RL decomposes those traces to identify the latent atomic modules and enable compositional generalization. We design controlled experiments to validate this theory. Our results demonstrate that RL can extract atomic modules from compound traces supplied by SFT and recombine them to solve new configurations. Moreover, we find that training on compound traces yields stronger generalization than training on isolated atomic modules. Finally, we investigate the relationship between SFT and RL data and identify an effective protocol in which SFT ensures coverage of all atomic modules through compositional traces, while RL focuses on novel compositions outside the SFT support to drive exploration.

</details>


### [88] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Minimum Zero-Forcing Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18106)
*Steve Halley,Maurício Gruppi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的框架SD-ZFS，用于求解无向图上的最小零强制集问题（NP-hard），通过适配S2V-DQN架构，实验表明该框架在最优解和贪婪启发式对比中表现有效，并分析了模型在不同图结构上的泛化、扩展和迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小零强制集问题在网络科学、网络控制和逻辑电路设计等领域有广泛应用，但已被证明是NP-hard问题，现有方法难以高效求解，因此需要新的机器学习方法来近似求解。

Method: 提出SD-ZFS框架，将S2V-DQN强化学习架构适配到零强制集问题，训练多个模型并在不同结构的图数据集上分析性能。

Result: 模型在最优解和贪婪启发式对比中表现有效，具备良好的泛化性、可扩展性和跨网络类型迁移能力。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法能够有效解决ZFS问题，网络结构对问题求解有显著影响，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: This paper explores the problem of finding the minimum zero-forcing set on undirected graphs and proposes an adapted machine-learning framework to solve the problem. The minimum zero-forcing set problem is a graph coloring problem where the color of an initial set of nodes propagates throughout a network. The set of nodes is zero-forcing if it forces all uncolored nodes to change color under the constraint of the color-change rule. There are several applications to this problem across different domains such as network science, network control, and designing logical circuits. Finding the minimum zero-forcing set is shown to be NP-hard. We propose a reinforcement learning framework, SD-ZFS, that adapts the S2V-DQN architecture to the ZFS problem. We train several models on this adapted framework and analyze the performance across graph datasets that have varying structures. We evaluate how the models trained on the framework generalize, scale, and transfer to different network types. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework when compared against the optimal solution and greedy heuristic. We provide further insight into how the ZFS problem can be solved through machine-learning and the influence of network structure on the problem.

</details>


### [89] [Learning Fair Pareto-Optimal Policies in Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18111)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多策略多目标强化学习（MORL）中的公平优化问题，提出了形式化定义和三种新算法，通过将广义基尼福利函数（GGF）与多策略MOQL相结合，并引入非平稳策略和随机策略，学习出一组公平的Pareto最优策略集，实验结果表明该方法优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单策略MORL方法虽能通过福利函数（如GGF）学习固定用户偏好下的公平策略，但无法提供动态或未知用户偏好所需的多样化策略集，因此需要将公平优化扩展到多策略MORL场景中。

Method: (1) 理论证明凹分段线性福利函数（如GGF）下的公平策略仍在凸覆盖集(CCS)中；(2) 提出非平稳策略（结合累积奖励历史增强）和随机策略可改善公平性；(3) 提出三种算法：GGF+多策略MOQL、状态增强的多策略MOQL（学习非平稳策略）、及其扩展（学习随机策略）。

Result: 在多个领域上的实验表明，所提方法能够学习出一组适应不同用户偏好的公平策略，性能优于现有MORL基线方法。

Conclusion: 将GGF等福利函数与多策略MORL结合，并利用非平稳/随机策略的状态增强机制，是解决多策略MORL中公平优化问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Fairness is an important aspect of decision-making in multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), where policies must ensure both optimality and equity across multiple, potentially conflicting objectives. While single-policy MORL methods can learn fair policies for fixed user preferences using welfare functions such as the generalized Gini welfare function (GGF), they fail to provide the diverse set of policies necessary for dynamic or unknown user preferences. To address this limitation, we formalize the fair optimization problem in multi-policy MORL, where the goal is to learn a set of Pareto-optimal policies that ensure fairness across all possible user preferences. Our key technical contributions are threefold: (1) We show that for concave, piecewise-linear welfare functions (e.g., GGF), fair policies remain in the convex coverage set (CCS), which is an approximated Pareto front for linear scalarization. (2) We demonstrate that non-stationary policies, augmented with accrued reward histories, and stochastic policies improve fairness by dynamically adapting to historical inequities. (3) We propose three novel algorithms, which include integrating GGF with multi-policy multi-objective Q-Learning (MOQL), state-augmented multi-policy MOQL for learning non-statoinary policies, and its novel extension for learning stochastic policies. We evaluate our algorithms across various domains and compare our methods against the state-of-the-art MORL baselines. The empirical results show that our methods learn a set of fair policies that accommodate different user preferences.

</details>


### [90] [Ternary Mamba: Grouped Quantization-Aware Training of W1.58A16 State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18114)
*Ramprasath Ganesaraja,Sahil Dilip Panse,Swathika N*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于预训练权重的三元量化感知训练（QAT）方法，仅需1亿token（约4GPU小时）即可将Mamba-2 1.3B模型压缩3.61倍（从2687MB降至744MB），达到48.1%的零样本准确率（7任务平均），接近从头训练的Bi-Mamba（48.4%），将token预算降低1000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM（如Mamba-2）推理复杂度为线性，但其内存占用限制了边缘部署。此前三元SSM工作（Slender-Mamba）需从头训练150B token，成本过高。本文旨在探索是否可以利用预训练checkpoint进行数据高效的量化压缩。

Method: 采用分组量化感知训练（QAT）结合知识蒸馏（KD），冻结FP16教师模型，对Mamba-2 1.3B进行三元量化压缩。仅需102M token（4 GPU-hours, 单H100）完成训练。

Result: 模型从2,687 MB压缩至744 MB（3.61倍），7任务零样本平均准确率48.1%（与Bi-Mamba的48.4%相差在±0.9pp置信区间内）。发现从预训练开始的QAT存在"零比例崩溃"新问题——可学习的量化尺度导致的不稳定性，而从零训练中不会出现。

Conclusion: 三元SSM不需要昂贵的从头训练，基于预训练checkpoint的QAT+KD是一种数据高效的替代方案。同时指出对Transformer有效的后处理校正策略因循环中的误差累积在SSM上失效。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) such as Mamba-2 offer linear-time inference but their memory footprint limits edge deployment. Prior ternary SSM work (Slender-Mamba) trains from scratch on 150B tokens; we show a pretrained checkpoint suffices, reducing the marginal token budget by 1,000x. Using grouped quantization-aware training (QAT) with knowledge distillation from a frozen FP16 teacher, we compress Mamba-2 1.3B to 3.61x (2,687 to 744 MB) and achieve 48.1% zero-shot accuracy (7-task average) in just 102M tokens (4 GPU-hours, single H100) -- approaching Bi-Mamba's 48.4% (within +/-0.9pp CI). This QAT-from-pretrained setting reveals zero-ratio collapse, a novel instability caused by learnable quantization scales that does not arise in from-scratch training. We further show that post-hoc correction strategies effective for Transformers fail for SSMs due to error accumulation through the recurrence. These results demonstrate that ternary SSMs do not require expensive from-scratch training: QAT from pretrained checkpoints with KD is a data-efficient alternative.

</details>


### [91] [Embedded Machine Learning for Microcontroller-Class Edge Devices: Data, Feature, Evaluation, and Deployment Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18122)
*Mostafa Darvishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了面向微控制器平台的嵌入式机器学习工作流，涵盖采样缓冲、特征提取、不平衡数据验证、模型/运行时协同设计及流式部署等关键工程决策，并通过惯性运动识别和关键词唤醒两个典型案例验证了设计规则的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式机器学习将推理从云端迁移到资源受限设备上，但这些设备在内存、能耗和延迟方面有严格限制，而通用的机器学习教程往往忽略了采样、缓冲、特征提取等关键工程决策，导致实际部署困难。

Method: 采用系统导向的综合方法，以惯性运动识别（三轴加速度计窗口→均方根+频谱特征）和关键词唤醒（音频采样→抗混叠→MFCC→一维卷积网络）两个代表性信号族为例，详细阐述嵌入式ML工作流的各环节工程决策。

Result: 提出了面向微控制器平台的嵌入式机器学习完整工作流，包括采样缓冲策略、特征提取作为降维、不平衡数据验证、模型与运行时协同设计、流式部署方案，以及数据整理、量化、阈值设置、调度和现场监控等实践设计规则。

Conclusion: 本文总结出实用的嵌入式设备稳健推理设计规则，为资源受限平台上的机器学习部署提供了系统化的工程指导。

Abstract: Embedded machine learning moves inference from cloud services to resource-constrained devices that must acquire data, preprocess signals, run a model, and act within tight limits on memory, energy, and latency. This paper presents a systems-oriented synthesis of an embedded machine-learning workflow for microcontroller-class platforms. The emphasis is placed on engineering decisions that are often hidden in generic machine-learning introductions: sampling and buffering, feature extraction as dimensionality reduction, validation under class imbalance, model/runtime co-design, and streaming deployment. Two representative signal families are used throughout the paper. The first is inertial motion recognition, where a two-second, three-axis accelerometer window is transformed from raw samples into root-mean-square and spectral features before classification. The second is keyword spotting, where audio is sampled, anti-aliased, transformed into mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, and processed by a compact one-dimensional convolutional network. The paper concludes with practical design rules for robust on-device inference, including data curation, quantization, thresholding, scheduling, and field monitoring.

</details>


### [92] [Kolmogorov Regression for Robust Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18186)
*Lekan Molu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于后向Kolmogorov方程的Cameron-Martin空间扩散策略，通过确定性边界值PDE替代随机分数匹配，利用高斯测度理论和有色噪声协方差算子实现正则化采样。在PushT操作基准和CONWIP生产线控制中，该方法在奖励、漂移抑制、瓶颈识别和死锁预防等方面取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维扩散策略因离散化伪影产生时间漂移，导致长期部署性能退化。现有方法缺乏对轨迹正则性的有效约束，亟需一种能够保证收敛性、提升轨迹平滑度且无需奖励信号的确定性诊断机制。

Method: 引入后向Kolmogorov方程将扩散策略提升至Cameron-Martin空间（Hilbert子空间）；用确定性边界值PDE替代随机分数匹配；基于高斯测度理论，利用有色噪声协方差算子实现样本正则性控制；提出精度加权的Cameron-Martin损失函数进行训练；引入Kolmogorov残差作为推理时PDE诊断工具。

Result: PushT基准：最大回合奖励提升17%（0.95 vs 0.78 MSE），推理时步间漂移降低67.6%。CONWIP产线：RMSE较LSTM降低28.4%，饥饿事件召回率1.0，瓶颈识别Precision@1=1.0，信噪比提升13倍。结合Hamilton-Jacobi可达性理论，死锁事件减少96%（351次预防）。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过函数空间正则化实现了收敛性保证（界常数依赖于核有效秩而非动作维度）、轨迹正则性改善和确定性故障检测，在操作控制和制造系统调度中均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Finite-dimensional (FD) diffusion policies exhibit temporal drift owing to discretization artifacts that degrade long-horizon performance (when deployed on physical systems). We introduce a backward Kolmogorov equation that lifts diffusion policies to a Cameron-Martin space -- a subset of the Hilbert space. Essentially, replacing stochastic score matching with a deterministic boundary-value PDE problem. Our core innovation thrives on Gaussian measure theory whereupon the diffusion noise covariance operator is realized from a colored noise distribution which prescribes a notion of regularity on samples from the model at inference time. We train the diffusion model with a derived precision-weighted Cameron- Martin loss and a Kolmogorov residual is introduced as a PDE diagnostic during inference. These substitutions yield (i) convergence guarantees where the bound's constants depend on the effective rank of the kernel rather than action dimension, (ii) improved trajectory regularity via spectral weighting, and (iii) a deterministic failure detector without reward signals. Validation across two application domains demonstrates substantial improvements: on the PushT manipulation benchmark, the Cameron-Martin loss achieves a 17% improvement in maximum episode reward (0.95 vs. 0.78 for MSE) and 67.6% reduction in inter-step drifts during inference via the introduced residual magnitude. Similarly, on a 6-station manufacturing line with constant work-in-process (CONWIP) flow control, we achieve 28.4% lower RMSE than classical LSTM baselines; a high starvation-event recall (1.0 in test cycles), and effective bottleneck identification (Precision@1 = 1.0 in test set, 13x signal-to-noise ratio). We then certify the dispatch policies with Hamilton-Jacobi reachability theory which reduces deadlock events by 96% compared to uncontrolled dispatch over 100 simulated runs (351 events prevented).

</details>


### [93] [Looped World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18208)
*Hongyuan Adam Lu,Z. L. Victor Wei,Qun Zhang,Jinrui Zeng,Bowen Cao,Lingwei Meng,Mocheng Li,Zezhong Wang,Haonan Yin,Naifu Xue,Minyu Chen,Cenyuan Zhang,Zefan Zhang,Hao Wei,Jiawei Zhou,Haoran Xu,Hao Yang,Ronglai Zuo,Tongda Xu,Yonghao Li,Jian Chen,Hebin Wang,Zeyu Gao,Yang Li,Wei Zhao,Qimin Zhong,Siqi Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Leyan Cui,Zhangyu Wang,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopWM提出循环世界模型架构，通过参数共享的Transformer块迭代优化潜在状态，在保持长时程高保真模拟的同时实现高达100倍参数效率，开辟了"迭代潜在深度"作为世界模型的新缩放维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型面临根本性矛盾：长时程高保真模拟需要深度计算，但深层模型部署成本高且易产生累积误差。作者希望解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出LoopWM，首个循环架构的世界模型。通过参数共享的Transformer块对潜在环境状态进行迭代精炼，实现自适应计算——每个预测步骤自动调整深度以匹配任务复杂度。

Result: 相比传统方法实现高达100倍参数效率，且自适应计算机制能根据预测步骤复杂度自动缩放深度。

Conclusion: LoopWM将迭代潜在深度确立为世界模拟的新缩放轴，正交于模型规模与训练数据量，有望推动该领域向前发展。

Abstract: Current world models face a fundamental tension: faithful long-horizon simulation demands deep computation, but deeper models are expensive to deploy and prone to compounding errors. We resolve this by introducing Looped World Models (LoopWM), which are the first looped architectures for world modelling. Our method iteratively refines latent environment states through a parameter-shared transformer block. This yield up to 100x parameter efficiency over conventional approaches with adaptive computation that automatically scales depth to match the complexity of each prediction step. Orthogonal to scaling model size and training data, LoopWM establishes iterative latent depth as a new scaling axis for world simulation, which might significantly push the community forward.

</details>


### [94] [Rethinking Dataset Distillation for Classification: Do Distilled Sets Outperform Coresets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18209)
*Trisha Mittal,Akshay Mehra,Joshua Kimball*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模标准化实验，系统评估了当前最先进的数据集蒸馏（DD）方法，发现其在ImageNet等大规模数据集上表现不如核心集选择（CS）方法，且构建成本更高。核心集在代表性、多样性和数据分布覆盖方面持续优于蒸馏数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法评估协议不统一（从标准ERM到单/多教师监督），难以分离蒸馏数据本身的有效性与评估方法的影响；同时许多方法声称DD优于数据剪枝（如核心集选择），但这些主张缺乏大规模标准化验证。

Method: 在ImageNet-1K、ImageNet100和ImageNette上，对七种SOTA DD方法使用三种广泛采用的训练协议，与三种CS策略进行对比；除了准确率外，还评估了蒸馏数据集/核心集在代表性、多样性和质量方面的表现。

Result: 部分DD方法无法超越简单随机子集；SOTA DD方法在大规模数据集上最多与核心集相当或更差，且构建成本显著更高；核心集在原始数据分布覆盖上持续优于蒸馏数据。

Conclusion: 当前DD方法实际优势有限，核心集仍是数据为中心学习中更具计算效率的竞争性选择。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) has emerged as a prominent approach in data centric machine learning, aiming to synthesize compact training sets for efficient training by compressing the information in large datasets into a small number of synthetic samples. However, DD methods are often evaluated under inconsistent evaluation protocols, ranging from standard ERM to single/multi-teacher supervision, making it difficult to isolate the effectiveness of distilled data from evaluation. Moreover, many prior methods claim that DD outperforms data pruning approaches such as coreset selection (CS), based on the assumption that restricting condensed datasets to subsets of real samples fundamentally limits their expressiveness. In this work, we critically evaluate DD methods through large-scale experiments using standardized datasets and evaluation protocols to assess their intrinsic effectiveness. We benchmark seven state-of-the-art (SOTA) DD methods on ImageNet-1K, ImageNet100, and ImageNette, using three widely adopted training protocols against three CS strategies. Our results show that while some DD methods fail to outperform even simple random subsets, the SOTA DD approaches are comparable to or worse than coresets on large-scale datasets and incur a substantially higher cost for construction. Beyond accuracy, we also evaluate the representativeness, diversity, and quality of condensed sets, and find that coresets consistently achieve better coverage of the original data distribution. These findings highlight the limited practical advantages of current DD methods and show that coresets remain competitive and are often a more computationally efficient alternative for data-centric learning.

</details>


### [95] [Sign-Rank, Index, and List Replicability: Connections and Separations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18236)
*Ari Blondal,Hamed Hatami,Pooya Hatami,Chavdar Lalov,Sivan Tretiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了概念类的符号秩（sign rank）下界问题，比较了两种下界度量——Z₂-指数和列表可复制数（list replicability number），证明了Z₂-指数被列表可复制数的线性函数所上界约束，从而解决了Frick、Hosseini和Vasileuski提出的关于符号秩与Z₂-指数之间强分离的问题。此外，通过高度（height）和最小星数（minimum star number）两种组合度量给出了列表可复制数的上界，并证明了列表可复制数在概念类乘积上满足次可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号秩的下界证明极为困难，而Z₂-指数和列表可复制数是两种较易分析的下界度量。本文旨在厘清这两种度量之间的序关系，并深入理解列表可复制数的性质。

Method: 通过理论分析，建立Z₂-指数与列表可复制数之间的不等式关系；利用高度和最小星数两种组合度量来上界约束列表可复制数；通过乘积构造证明列表可复制数的次可加性。

Result: Z₂-指数被列表可复制数的线性函数上界约束；利用高度和最小星数给出了列表可复制数的上界；证明了列表可复制数在概念类乘积上的次可加性。

Conclusion: 本文阐明了Z₂-指数与列表可复制数的关系，解决了符号秩与Z₂-指数强分离的公开问题，并为列表可复制数建立了组合上界和乘积性质，推动了符号秩下界研究的发展。

Abstract: In learning theory, the sign rank of a binary concept class captures the smallest dimension in which it can be represented by points and halfspaces. Despite tremendous interest, lower bounds on sign rank are notoriously difficult to come by. Two recent approaches to the problem establish lower bounds on sign rank by measures that are easier to analyze: the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index and the list replicability number.
  We order these measures, showing that the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index is upper-bounded by a linear function of the list replicability number. As a main consequence, we obtain a strong separation between sign rank and $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index, thereby resolving a question of Frick, Hosseini, and Vasileuski.
  This motivates a thorough study of list replicability, the stronger of the two lower-bounding measures. We establish upper bounds on the list replicability number by two combinatorial measures: height and minimum star number. We also prove a fundamental composition result, showing that the product of two concept classes has list replicability number bounded by the sum of the list replicability numbers of the two classes.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Evaluating LLM Coding Agents on SZ-Family Lossy Compression Across Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17058)
*Changqing Li,Shouwei Gao,Kai Zhao,Sheng Di,Wenqian Dong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM编码智能体在SZ系列误差有界压缩核上的表现，发现在GPU和Cerebras晶圆级加速器上存在显著的跨架构差异：GPU上强模型吞吐量更高但对提示更敏感，Cerebras上主要挑战是生成可运行程序；LLM智能体在模块化核(SZx)上比在紧耦合位级流水线(SZp)上更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM编码智能体在高性能计算场景中的有效性缺乏系统评估，尤其是在性能关键型HPC环境中的跨架构表现尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 评估LLM编码工作流在SZ系列误差有界压缩核上的表现，研究两个代表性CUDA工作负载(SZp和SZx)在两个异构执行平台(NVIDIA GPU和Cerebras晶圆级加速器)上的单智能体迭代生成过程，分析吞吐量、迭代模式、提示敏感性和失败模式。

Result: GPU上强模型吞吐量更高但对提示精度和优化指导更敏感；Cerebras上主要挑战是生成可运行程序；LLM智能体在模块化核(SZx)上比在紧耦合位级流水线(SZp)上更有效，结构依赖会阻碍优化进展。

Conclusion: 评估LLM编码智能体在HPC中的表现需同时考虑性能结果和架构特定鲁棒性，在基于线程的平台上的成功不能直接迁移到空间加速器。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents are increasingly applied to code translation and optimization, yet their effectiveness in performance-critical high-performance computing (HPC) settings remains poorly characterized. This paper evaluates LLM-based coding workflows on SZ-family error-bounded lossy compression kernels, which combine numerical constraints with memory-intensive and control-flow-heavy implementations. We study two representative CUDA workloads (SZp and SZx) and target two heterogeneous execution platforms: NVIDIA GPUs and Cerebras wafer-scale accelerators. Focusing on single-agent iterative generation, we analyze not only final throughput but also agent runtime behavior, including iteration patterns, sensitivity to prompt specification, and characteristic failure modes. Our results reveal a pronounced cross-architecture divergence. On GPUs, stronger models can achieve substantially higher throughput but exhibit increased sensitivity to prompt precision and optimization guidance, whereas on Cerebras the dominant challenge lies in producing runnable programs under a PE-centric spatial execution model. We further observe that LLM agents are more effective on modular kernels (SZx) than on tightly coupled bit-level pipelines (SZp), where structural dependencies hinder optimization progress. These findings suggest that evaluating LLM coding agents for HPC requires accounting for both performance outcomes and architecture-specific robustness, and that success on thread-based platforms does not directly transfer to spatial accelerators.

</details>


### [97] [Towards Distributed Inference of LLMs on a P2P Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17059)
*Shabari S Nair,Krishanu Saini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种去中心化的前缀缓存感知路由方案，用于对等LLM服务。每个节点维护本地基数树和异步刷新的对等缓存估计，请求被路由到估计前缀匹配最长的节点，无需集中协调或KV缓存传输。实验表明，在低通信延迟和偏斜前缀分布下，去中心化路由能改善延迟，但高网络延迟和亲和性热点会限制其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存可以重用KV缓存来降低LLM推理延迟，但在集群规模下缓存被分区到不同节点，现有方案面临跨节点缓存共享和路由的挑战。

Method: 提出去中心化的前缀缓存感知路由方案：每个节点维护本地基数树记录自身缓存前缀，通过周期性反熵异步刷新对等节点缓存估计；请求被路由到估计前缀匹配最长的节点，无需集中协调或KV缓存传输；采用弱一致性保证正确性。

Result: 在模拟MMLU工作负载上评估，去中心化路由在低通信延迟和偏斜前缀分布下能改善延迟；但高网络延迟和亲和性引起的热点会限制其优势。

Conclusion: 去中心化前缀缓存感知路由在特定条件下有效，但网络延迟和热点问题是其实际部署的关键限制因素。

Abstract: Prefix caching can reduce LLM inference latency by reusing KV caches across requests with shared prompts, but cluster-scale reuse is challenging because caches are partitioned across nodes. We propose a decentralized, prefix-cache-aware routing scheme for peer-to-peer LLM serving. Each node maintains a local radix tree of its own cached prefixes and asynchronously refreshed estimates of peer caches using periodic anti-entropy. Requests are routed to the node with the longest estimated prefix match, without centralized coordination or KV-cache transfer. Stale metadata only causes cache misses, not incorrect outputs, making weak consistency sufficient for correctness. Evaluation on simulated MMLU workloads show that decentralized routing improves latency under low communication delay and skewed prefix distributions, while high network latency and affinity-induced hotspots limit its benefits.

</details>


### [98] [Local Fault Repair of Perfect Resource Placements in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17288)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络中资源故障后的局部修复问题，证明了单故障需要2个替换资源（最优），双故障在某些情况下可仅用3个替换资源（非可加性），并给出了多故障修复的通用上界和精确修复数，发现了与高斯情况显著不同的结构特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络中，资源放置将网络划分为六边形服务单元。当资源发生故障时，需要研究如何用最少的替换资源恢复覆盖，以及多故障场景下的修复效率优化问题。

Method: 通过几何分析（三带结构、两球不可行论证）、代数方法（端点刚性定理、对角走廊定理）、可加性分析、包含-排除恒等式，以及对19,400个实例的审计验证。

Result: 单故障修复数ρ_EJ(t)=2；最小修复重叠公式Ω_EJ(t)=t²；双故障存在非可加的三替换修复；多故障通用上界2q；密集簇修复可实现4和5的精确修复数，分别节省4和7个替换资源。

Conclusion: EJ局部修复在结构上不同于高斯情况：单故障重叠是二次的，双故障修复可以是不可加的，群集修复可跨多个故障单元重用替换球。

Abstract: Perfect resource placements in dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks partition the network into hexagonal radius-$t$ service cells. This paper studies local repair of such placements after resource failures. For one failed resource, we prove that one replacement cannot cover the failed hexagon and two always suffice, giving $ρ_{\mathrm{EJ}}(t)=2$ for all $t\ge1$. Among minimum-size repairs, the sharp minimum-overlap formula $Ω_{\mathrm{EJ}}(t)=t^2$ follows from the three-strip geometry of EJ balls. For two failed resources, independent repair gives a four-replacement upper bound, but unlike the Gaussian case EJ repair is not always additive: two infinite neighboring displacement families admit three-replacement repairs, proved optimal by a two-ball impossibility argument. Additive behavior is established algebraically via endpoint-rigidity and diagonal-corridor theorems. For $q$ failed resources, independent canonical repair gives a universal $2q$ upper bound, exact when failed cells are pairwise more than $4t$ apart. Dense cluster subadditivity is proved for infinite four-fault and six-fault families with exact repair numbers four and five, giving savings of four and seven over independent repair. An exact inclusion--exclusion identity governs repeated coverage for arbitrary multi-fault repairs. An audit over 19,400 instances confirms widespread subadditivity. EJ local repair is structurally distinct from the Gaussian case: the one-fault overlap is quadratic, two-fault repair can be non-additive, and clustered repairs reuse replacement balls across multiple failed cells.

</details>


### [99] [Space-Efficient Lock-Free Linear-Probing Hash Table](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17315)
*Hagit Attiya,Rotem Oshman,Noa Schiller*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种无锁线性探测哈希表，支持无等待查找操作，仅需少量元数据即可实现强活性保证，且操作的平均步数复杂度与顺序线性探测相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发线性探测哈希表难以同时保证强活性（如无锁、无等待）和空间效率，已有算法要么限制并发度，要么依赖大量每条目元数据从而牺牲空间效率。

Method: 设计了一种无锁线性探测哈希表，每表条目仅需少量额外比特（LL/SC下常数比特，CAS下对数比特），支持线性化、无锁插入/删除和无等待查找，并能安全回收已删除元素空间而无需重建表。

Result: 证明了在无相同键并发插入假设下，各操作的期望平摊步数复杂度与顺序线性探测一致，达到每键争用点的理论上界。

Conclusion: 该算法在保留顺序线性探测核心优势（紧凑内存布局、高空间效率）的同时，实现了强并发保证，是首个在极小元数据开销下达到无锁+无等待的线性探测哈希表设计。

Abstract: Linear probing is one of the simplest and most space-efficient approaches to hash table design, and is widely used in sequential settings due to its compact memory layout. However, designing a concurrent linear-probing hash table with strong liveness guarantees has proved difficult, and only a handful of such algorithms have been proposed, all of which either restrict concurrency or rely on large per-entry metadata, thereby compromising space efficiency.
  We present a lock-free linear-probing hash table with wait-free lookups that retains the core advantages of sequential linear probing while handling contention gracefully. Our design uses only a small amount of metadata per table entry: a constant number of additional bits when using LL/SC, or a logarithmic number of bits when using CAS. The algorithm is linearizable and lock-free, supports insert, delete, and wait-free lookup operations, and is able to safely reclaim space used by deleted elements without rebuilding the table.
  We analyze the amortized step complexity of our hash table assuming no concurrent insertions of the same key, and show that each operation has expected amortized step complexity matching that of sequential linear probing, up to the point contention per key.

</details>


### [100] [RISE: Relay Inference and Online Scheduling for Efficient Edge-Device Collaborative Diffusion Model Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17378)
*Zilan Huang,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Tian Wang,Yuan Wu,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RISE提出了一种结合接力推理与在线调度的边缘-设备协同扩散模型服务方法。大模型在边缘端处理早期去噪步骤塑造语义结构，小模型在设备端进行细节优化，并通过上下文感知调度器平衡质量与延迟，在保持全模型质量的同时实现高达2.1倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘部署策略要么使用大模型追求高保真度但延迟高，要么使用轻量级模型追求速度但牺牲语义一致性，且缺乏跨边缘服务器和用户设备的不同大小模型之间的去噪工作负载拆分方案。

Method: RISE包含两个核心组件：1）训练无关的接力机制，利用同一模型家族内的共享潜在空间，大模型处理早期去噪步骤后传递中间潜在表示给小模型进行细节优化；2）上下文赌博机调度器，根据提示复杂度、用户偏好、网络质量和节点实时负载选择最佳接力配置。

Result: 在两个基准测试上，接力机制实现高达2.1倍加速同时保持全模型质量；上下文感知调度器在混合工作负载下有效平衡质量与延迟。

Conclusion: RISE通过边缘-设备接力推理和在线调度，有效弥合了边缘扩散模型服务中质量与延迟之间的权衡鸿沟。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models are increasingly deployed at the network edge to serve heterogeneous workloads with diverse quality and latency requirements. However, existing deployment strategies choose either large edge-side models with high fidelity but high latency or lightweight device-side models that offer speed at the cost of semantic coherence. Moreover, these approaches rarely split the denoising workload between models of different sizes across edge servers and user devices. To bridge this gap, we propose RISE, a method for edge-device diffusion model services that combines relay inference with online scheduling. Driven by the finding that the latent intensity exhibits minimal deviation after a model handoff, RISE uses a training-free relay mechanism that exploits the shared latent space within a model family: the large model on the edge handles the early denoising steps that shape semantic structure, then passes the intermediate latent to a small device-side model for detail refinement. To deploy this mechanism as a practical service, a contextual bandit scheduler selects the best relay configuration based on prompt complexity, user preferences, network quality and real-time node loads. Experiments on two benchmarks show that RISE's relay mechanism achieves up to 2.1$\times$ speedup while preserving full-model quality, and its context-aware scheduler effectively balances quality and latency under mixed workloads.

</details>


### [101] [SpecGen: Accelerating Agentic Kernel Optimization with Speculative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17518)
*Jihu Guo,Sitian Lu,Tenghui Ma,Wei Gao,Zhisheng Ye,Xingcheng Zhang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SpecGen 是一个基于推测生成的智能体内核优化系统，通过在推理过程中提前分叉非推理生成、动态资源重分配和远程 KV 缓存复用，解决 LLM 驱动内核优化中的生成延迟长、反馈不足和资源利用率低三大效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理 LLM 的智能体内核优化存在三个系统级低效问题：(1) LLM 推理导致的长生成延迟；(2) 分析反馈不足；(3) 验证/分析资源利用率低。核心洞察是：正在进行的推理生成为产生额外候选内核提供了时间窗口，允许在满意内核出现时提前终止推理。

Method: (1) 推测生成：在推理轨迹中选取触发点分叉非推理生成，并行产生产出候选内核并进行验证和分析，满足条件时提前终止推理；(2) 动态 GPU 资源重分配：根据到达率动态调整验证和分析 GPU 池，优先处理请求以减少突发推测负载下的反馈延迟；(3) 远程 KV 缓存复用：利用验证/分析 GPU 的空闲内存作为远程 KV 缓存存储，消除推测生成的 prefix 重计算。

Result: 在 H200 上使用两种推理 LLM 的实验表明，SpecGen 相比三种基线系统减少了端到端时间，同时产生了更多分析反馈，提高了资源利用率，并在固定时间和 token 预算下提升了内核加速比。

Conclusion: SpecGen 通过推测生成机制有效解决了智能体内核优化中的系统效率问题，为 LLM 驱动的自动化内核调优提供了一种高效的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic kernel optimization automates manual GPU kernel tuning via iterative generation, validation, and profiling with reasoning LLMs, casting the optimization task as feedback-guided search. However, our workload characterization reveals three system-level inefficiencies that limit search efficiency: (1) long generation latency due to LLM reasoning, (2) insufficient profiling feedback, and (3) underutilized validation/profiling resources. Our key insight is that the ongoing reasoning generation exposes a window for producing additional candidate kernels before it completes, allowing the system to terminate reasoning early once a satisfactory kernel appears.
  We present SpecGen, an agentic kernel optimization system with \emph{speculative generation}. First, SpecGen forks non-reasoning generations at well-chosen trigger points in the reasoning trace to yield kernels, increasing the candidate kernel count per iteration. These kernels are validated and profiled in parallel with the ongoing reasoning, increasing profiling feedback, and keeping resources busy during generation. When a kernel meets the termination criterion, SpecGen terminates the reasoning generation early to reduce the generation latency. Second, SpecGen dynamically reallocates validation and profiling GPU pools based on the arrival rate and prioritizes requests to reduce profiling feedback latency under bursty speculative generation load. Furthermore, SpecGen utilizes spare memory of the validation/profiling GPUs as remote KV cache storage to eliminate prefix recomputation of speculative generations under limited memory budget. Experiments with two reasoning LLMs on H200 show that SpecGen reduces end-to-end time over three baseline systems, while producing more profiling feedback, increasing resource utilization, and improving kernel speedup under a fixed time and token budget.

</details>


### [102] [Local Fault Repair of Perfect Resource Placements in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17527)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究稠密高斯网络中资源放置的修复问题，给出了精确的局部修复定理：单个资源故障的替换数为ρ_G(1)=3和ρ_G(t≥2)=2，最小重叠数为Ω_G(t)=t+1；两个资源故障需要恰好4个局部替换；多故障修复满足容斥恒等式。通过7,494个高斯案例的实证验证了所有公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稠密高斯网络的资源放置中，无故障放置问题已有分类，但资源发生故障后的修复问题尚未被研究。本文旨在解决这一后部署修复问题，为高斯网络中的资源故障提供精确的局部修复理论。

Method: 针对由生成元t+(t+1)i及其共轭(t+1)+ti生成的稠密高斯放置，利用共轭和旋转对称性，通过旋转坐标u=x+y和v=x-y将高斯Lee球转化为奇偶约束正方形，分析其角结构；对单故障、双故障和多故障情况分别推导精确替换数和重叠公式；并通过7,494个高斯案例进行地面实况审计验证。

Result: 单故障：ρ_G(1)=3，ρ_G(t≥2)=2，最小重叠Ω_G(t)=t+1。双故障：每个故障单元对恰好需要4个局部替换（t≥2时）。多故障：满足容斥恒等式，当最大重数为3时简化为Ω_extra=P_2-A-C_3。7,494个案例验证了所有公式。

Conclusion: 本文为稠密高斯网络资源放置的修复问题建立了完整的精确局部修复理论，包括单故障、双故障和多故障的替换数及重叠公式，并通过大规模数值实验验证了理论结果的正确性。

Abstract: Perfect resource placement in dense Gaussian networks partitions the network into Lee balls centered at resource nodes. The fault-free placement problem is already classified; this paper studies the complementary post-deployment problem of repairing such placements after resource faults. The paper gives exact local repair theorems for the dense Gaussian placement generated by $t+(t+1)i$; by conjugation and rotation symmetry, the same results hold for the companion generator $(t+1)+ti$. For one failed resource, we prove failure-cell locality, derive the exact replacement number $ρ_G(1)=3$ and $ρ_G(t)=2$ for all $t\ge2$, and prove the sharp minimum-overlap formula $Ω_G(t)=t+1$ among minimum-size repairs. The overlap lower bound is proved from the corner structure of equal-size Lee balls in the rotated coordinates $u=x+y$ and $v=x-y$, where Gaussian Lee balls become parity-constrained squares. For two failed resources, we prove exact additivity: every pair of failed resource cells requires exactly four local replacements for $t\ge2$, and four always suffice. The two-fault lower bound reduces all relevant resource displacements to two canonical neighboring cases and exhibits four mutually incompatible failed-cell corners in each case. For multi-failure repairs, we prove a general inclusion--exclusion identity for overlap inside the failed region; hence the formula remains exact for arbitrary higher-order dense cores. When a canonical repair instance is certified to have maximum multiplicity three, the identity reduces to the compact correction $Ω_{\rm extra}=P_2-A-C_3$. A ground-truth audit over 7,494 Gaussian cases recomputes coverage from lattice geometry, verifies all exact formulas, and records reproducible multiplicity witnesses.

</details>


### [103] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17528)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对稠密高斯网络中的单源广播容错修复问题，提出多方向边最小修复方法（MOEM），通过选择容错感知的广播树方向、剪枝故障节点并利用最少的外部修复边重构健康生成树，保证非冗余广播，并在单/双故障下证明修复深度不超过k+2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密高斯网络是度为4的代数互联网络，具有紧致直径和模块化路由特性。在实际应用中，节点可能发生故障，需要在不使用故障节点的前提下，高效修复广播树以保证每个健康节点恰好接收一次消息。

Method: 提出多方向边最小修复（MOEM）方法：评估一族常数的广播树方向，选择容错感知的方向；对故障剪枝后的树进行连通分量收缩；利用跨分量的外部修复边以最少数量（c-1条）将分量重新连接，形成健康子图上的有根生成树。

Result: 理论证明：在选定的方向下，若有c个故障剪枝分量且健康分量图连通，则修复非冗余且使用最少c-1条外部修复边；对任意单/双故障，MOEM方向族中存在修复深度不超过k+2的解。实验验证：k=5~10的穷举验证及k=200的大规模验证证实实现正确性，随机双故障修复约使用2条外部修复边。

Conclusion: MOEM方法在稠密高斯网络中实现了最优边数的非冗余广播故障修复，理论深度上界与实验验证共同证明了该方法的高效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Dense Gaussian networks are degree-four algebraic interconnection networks with compact diameter and simple modular routing. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense Gaussian network generated by $α=k+(k+1)i$. We propose multi-orientation edge-minimum repair (MOEM), which evaluates a constant-size family of Gaussian broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware orientation, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects those components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph, so each healthy node receives the message exactly once and no faulty node is used. We prove that, for a chosen orientation with $c$ fault-pruned components and a connected healthy component graph, the repair step is non-redundant and uses the minimum possible number $c-1$ of external component-repair edges. We also prove that, for every one- or two-fault placement, the MOEM orientation family contains a repair with depth at most $k+2$. The depth proof combines a certificate framework, an explicit four-case off-axis analysis, and a five-component orthogonal-axis certificate. Exhaustive validation for $k=5,\ldots,10$ and large-scale validation through $k=200$ confirm the implementation and show that random two-fault repairs use approximately two external repair edges.

</details>


### [104] [AoiZora: Topology-Aware Auto-Parallel Optimization for Inference of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17566)
*Kaijian Wang,Yuanyuan Xu,Fanjiang Ye,Ye Cao,Jingwei Zuo,T. S. Eugene Ng,Yarong Mu,Yuke Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AoiZora是一个面向TPU子切片上低延迟视频扩散推理的编译器介导拓扑规划器，通过重新连接逻辑分片与物理布局，在TPU v5e子切片上将Wan 2.1单步去噪延迟最多降低1.42倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动并行系统几乎只搜索逻辑设备网格，忽略所选分片在物理TPU互连上的实际布局，导致大量拓扑相关性能未被利用。

Method: 通过在不同编译流程节点介入：先通过廉价的预编译IR剔除弱分片候选，再编译幸存候选并使用编译后的HLO结合拓扑感知通信模型确定物理布局，最终沿普通编译器路径实现最优方案，不修改模型代码、编译器降级、集合内核和网络路由。

Result: 在TPU v5e子切片上，AoiZora将Wan 2.1单步去噪延迟相比现有方案降低最多1.42倍。

Conclusion: 逻辑分片与物理布局的重新连接能显著提升视频扩散推理性能，且该方法无需改动现有编译链路。

Abstract: Video diffusion has quickly grown into a key generative serving workload, yet producing each clip demands many denoising iterations over large spatio-temporal latents, which puts low-latency inference out of reach on a single device. A denoising step is therefore typically distributed across multiple accelerators, and TPU sub-slices have become an attractive and practical fabric for doing so. Current auto-parallel systems, however, search almost exclusively over logical device meshes and disregard how a chosen sharding is actually laid out on the physical TPU interconnect -- an oversight that leaves large, topology-dependent performance on the table. We address this gap with AoiZora, a compiler-mediated topology planner built for low-latency video diffusion inference on TPU sub-slices. Its guiding principle is to reconnect logical sharding with physical placement by drawing on different points in the compilation flow: AoiZora first eliminates weak sharding candidates from inexpensive pre-compilation IRs, then compiles only the ones that survive and orders their physical placements using compiled HLO together with a topology-aware communication model. The winning plan is realized along the ordinary compiler path, leaving model code, compiler lowering, collective kernels, and network routing entirely intact. On TPU v5e sub-slices, AoiZora reduces Wan 2.1 one-step denoising latency by as much as 1.42x relative to existing solutions.

</details>


### [105] [From GPU to Microcontroller: Online Ridge Regression for Edge-Deployable Traffic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17613)
*Suresh Purini,Archit Narwadkar,Deepak Gangadharan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文发现现有交通流预测模型存在严重的参数冗余，提出用每传感器独立的Ridge回归+递归最小二乘在线自适应替代神经网络，在ESP32微控制器上即可运行，性能与SOTA相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA交通流预测模型（GCN、MLP等）依赖GPU集中训练，无法部署在资源受限的边缘设备上，实际部署成本高、实用性差。

Method: 1) 对GLMST进行参数分析，发现嵌入维度从64降到4性能损失极小；2) 用每传感器的Ridge回归+水平对齐周期特征替代神经网络；3) 使用递归最小二乘(RLS)实现在线自适应更新。

Result: 每传感器仅444参数（比GLMST少80倍）；在4个PEMS基准中3个取得最佳MAPE，第4个误差在1%以内；ESP32上冷启动训练7.4s，预测+更新<2ms；树莓派5上冷启动0.21s，预测+更新0.26ms。

Conclusion: 证明了交通流预测不需要复杂神经网络，简单的每传感器线性模型+在线自适应即可达到SOTA性能，且可在无GPU的边缘设备上高效运行。

Abstract: State-of-the-art traffic flow forecasting models, including Graph Convolutional Networks and graph-less MLPs, require centralized GPU training across all sensors, making them impractical for resource-constrained intelligent transportation deployments. We show that much of this complexity is unnecessary. A parametric analysis of the recent graph-less model GLMST reveals that reducing its internal embedding dimension from 64 to 4 degrades MAPE by less than one percentage point, suggesting that the model's effective capacity far exceeds what the task requires. Motivated by this finding, we replace the neural architecture entirely with per-sensor Ridge regression using horizon-aligned periodic features, combined with Recursive Least Squares (RLS) for online adaptation. With only 444 parameters per sensor (80x fewer than GLMST) and test-time online adaptation, our method achieves the best MAPE on three of four PEMS benchmarks, and remains within one percentage point on the fourth. Because each sensor's model is self-contained and involves only elementary linear algebra, the entire pipeline (training, inference, and online adaptation) runs on edge hardware without a GPU. An ESP32 microcontroller (160 MHz, 520 KB SRAM) completes cold-start training in 7.4s and each predict-and-update in under 2ms with zero heap allocation; a single Raspberry Pi 5 core completes cold-start training in 0.21s and each predict-and-update in 0.26ms.

</details>


### [106] [LUMEN: Coordinated Failure Recovery for Distributed LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17787)
*Zhang Cao,Shujie Han,Juncheng Zhang,Yuanming Ren,Yongkun Li,Patrick P. C. Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LUMEN是一种面向大语言模型服务集群的容错系统，通过在故障前检查点放置、故障时中断请求分配和模型重载期间服务容量恢复三个决策点进行负载感知协调，显著提升了服务质量和恢复时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务集群中工作节点故障频繁，导致KV缓存丢失和计算容量损失；现有容错系统（如从头重启或从固定邻居检查点恢复）未考虑集群负载情况，且在模型重载期间恢复节点处于空闲状态。

Method: 将恢复问题建模为负载感知的协调问题，涵盖三个关键决策点：1) 故障前的检查点放置策略；2) 故障时中断请求的分配策略；3) 模型重载期间服务容量的恢复策略。

Result: 通过原型实验和大规模仿真验证，LUMEN在服务时间和恢复时间上均取得显著改进。

Conclusion: LUMEN通过负载感知的协调机制有效提升了LLM服务集群在故障场景下的容错性能。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) serving clusters distribute inference requests across multiple worker processes on different GPUs, but failures are prevalent at scale. When a worker fails, the cluster simultaneously loses the failed worker's GPU-resident key-value (KV) caches and serving capacity, leaving surviving workers to absorb the redirected traffic while re-running interrupted requests from scratch. Existing fault-tolerant systems either restart interrupted requests from scratch or restore KV caches from checkpoints stored on a fixed neighboring worker, but both approaches route recovery work without considering current cluster load and leave the recovering worker idle during model reload. We present LUMEN, a fault-tolerant LLM serving system that treats recovery as a load-aware coordination problem across three decision points: checkpoint placement before failures, interrupted-request distribution at failure time, and serving capacity restoration during model reload. We evaluate LUMEN using both prototype experiments and large-scale simulations and demonstrate significant improvements in serving and recovery times.

</details>


### [107] [An Epistemic Analysis of Random Coordinated Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17860)
*Sophia Knight,David Lehnherr,Sergio Rajsbaum*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于研究有限轮次随机化算法的概率认知逻辑框架，适用于协调攻击、近似一致和共识问题。该框架结合了动态网络的逻辑表征与概率动态认知逻辑的技术，为随机化分布式计算的认知研究奠定了基础。通过该框架，作者从知识论视角分析了Varghese-Lynch算法，形式化处理了该算法及其下界，强化了下界并证明其紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化版本的协调攻击问题是可解的，但尚未从概率认知逻辑的角度进行研究。现有研究缺乏一个统一的逻辑框架来形式化分析随机化分布式算法中的知识和概率推理。

Method: 将动态网络和任务可解性的逻辑表征技术与概率动态认知逻辑相结合，提出了"概率认知任务可解性"概念。框架支持同步动态图模型，其中可靠进程执行有限轮次，对手决定消息丢失。

Result: 1) 建立了概率认知逻辑框架，适用于协调攻击、近似一致和共识问题。2) 从知识论视角形式化分析了Varghese-Lynch随机化协调攻击算法，提供了算法及其下界的严格形式化处理。3) 强化了该算法的下界并证明其紧致性。4) 形式化了Varghese和Lynch引入的"信息层级"概念，证明其对应于特定的认知公式。

Conclusion: 知识推理在概率化分布式计算环境中仍然具有核心作用。概率认知任务可解性为随机化分布式算法的认知研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The coordinated attack problem models the challenge of coordinating a joint action within a bounded time by communicating over unreliable links. It was the first distributed computing problem proven unsolvable. Its analysis also revealed the importance of common knowledge, a central concept in epistemic logic. However, the randomized version of coordinated attack, which is solvable, has not, to the best of our knowledge, been studied through the lens of probabilistic epistemic logic, where processes generate randomness by flipping coins.
  We present an epistemic logic framework for studying randomized algorithms that execute for a bounded number of rounds. The framework applies to coordinated attack, approximate agreement, and consensus, and supports dynamic graph models: synchronous systems in which reliable processes execute a bounded number of rounds while an adversary determines which messages are lost.
  Our approach combines techniques from the logical characterization of dynamic networks and task solvability with ideas from probabilistic dynamic epistemic logic. It is inspired by the operational model of Varghese and Lynch for randomized coordinated attack. More broadly, the resulting notion of probabilistic epistemic task solvability provides a foundation for the epistemic study of randomized distributed computation.
  Using this framework, we analyze the Varghese-Lynch algorithm from a knowledge-theoretic perspective, providing a formal treatment of the algorithm and its lower bound. As a byproduct, we strengthen the lower bound and show it is tight. The proof relies on indistinguishability arguments, demonstrating that reasoning about knowledge remains essential in the probabilistic setting. We also formalize the notion of information level introduced by Varghese and Lynch, showing that it corresponds to a specific epistemic formula.

</details>


### [108] [RouteBalance: Fused Model Routing and Load Balancing for Heterogeneous LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17949)
*Wei Da,Evangelia Kalyvianaki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RouteBalance 是一个面向异构LLM服务集群的调度层，将模型路由和负载均衡融合为单层联合决策，在质量、延迟和成本三者间进行权衡。在13实例、28 GPU的集群上部署，单个权重向量即可同时达到最优路由决策质量（DeepEval 0.419，比最强基线高+0.013）和最低成本端点。平衡预设下达到2.8秒延迟和30 req/s吞吐量，高负载下领先增强版BEST-Route 2.6-4.1倍。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构LLM服务堆栈将调度分为两层独立优化：模型路由器仅根据质量和成本选择模型而忽略实例负载，负载均衡器仅优化队列而忽略质量。这种隔离优化无法实现质量-延迟-成本的联合最优。

Method: 提出RouteBalance，将模型路由和负载均衡融合为单一在线分配层，直接针对具体模型实例进行联合决策。采用批处理进程内预测器栈和航位推测（dead-reckoned）实例状态，将请求热路径上的决策开销控制在约32ms（12 req/s）。

Result: 在13实例、28 GPU异构集群上，单一路由栈即可覆盖质量-成本-吞吐量三维前沿的上界。单个权重向量同时达到最优路由决策质量（DeepEval 0.419，+0.013）和最低成本。平衡预设下2.8秒延迟、30 req/s吞吐量，高负载时领先增强版BEST-Route 2.6-4.1倍。

Conclusion: 联合调度带来的核心收益在于在模型选择阶段对延迟进行定价；学习的预测器主要用于校准和SLO余量，而非直接贡献前沿性能。将延迟代价纳入模型选择是提升异构LLM服务整体效率的关键。

Abstract: Heterogeneous LLM serving stacks split scheduling into two layers that optimize in isolation: model routers pick a model from quality and cost signals while ignoring instance load, and serving load balancers optimize queues while ignoring quality. We present RouteBalance, a serving-aware scheduling layer that fuses both into a single online assignment over concrete model instances, jointly trading off quality, latency, and cost. A batched in-process predictor stack and dead-reckoned instance state keep the joint decision cheap on the request hot path ($\approx$32 ms at 12 req/s). On a 13-instance, 28-GPU heterogeneous cluster serving four model sizes, a single deployed RouteBalance stack traces the upper region of the three-way quality-cost-throughput frontier. Sweeping one weight vector reaches both the highest routing-decision quality (DeepEval $0.419$, $+0.013$ over the strongest baseline, $95\%$ CI $[{+}0.005,{+}0.022]$; the ordering holds when a second judge re-scores the actually served text) and, at its cost-priority corner, per-request cost that ties the cheapest baseline. With router engineering equalized against concurrent-scoring baseline variants we build, its balanced preset serves at $2.8$ s and $30$ req/s, leading $2.6$ to $4.1\times$ ahead of enhanced BEST-Route at high load. (Deploying those routers as published, one serial scoring call per request, makes them collapse $23\times$ under load, a deployment-architecture effect we isolate separately, not the routing result.) A four-arm isolation shows the benefit follows from pricing latency at model-selection time; the learned predictors contribute calibration and SLO headroom rather than the headline frontier. Code: https://github.com/AKafakA/route-balance

</details>


### [109] [Latency Prediction for LLM Inference on NPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18042)
*Juhyun Park,Seungwoo Jeong,Jingyu Lee,Kyungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LENS是一种无需微架构或编译器信息即可预测NPU上LLM推理延迟的估计器，通过每个bucket两次端到端测量并组合结果，在多种NPU、LLM和工作负载上实现2.15%的平均预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署LLM需要探索包含并行化策略、批处理技术和调度策略的巨大配置空间，穷举测量不可行，因此需要延迟预测来优化系统。然而现有预测方法不适用于NPU，面临三大挑战：商业NPU微架构不公开、编译器优化不可预测、以及bucketing导致的延迟非线性。

Method: LENS对每个bucket进行两次端到端测量，然后将这些测量结果组合起来，预测任意输入-输出长度组合下的延迟。该方法无需微架构或编译器信息，并能捕获bucketing导致的非线性延迟。

Result: 在多个供应商的NPU、多种LLM和多样化工作负载上验证，平均预测误差为2.15%。与两个方法学相关的基线对比，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: LENS成功解决了NPU上LLM推理延迟预测的三个核心挑战（微架构不公开、编译器优化不可预测、bucketing非线性），提供了一种高精度、通用且无需硬件内部细节的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) requires exploring a large configuration space spanning parallelization strategies, batching techniques, and scheduling policies. Exhaustive measurement across this space is impractical, making latency prediction essential for system optimization. While NPUs have emerged as accelerators designed for LLM inference, no prediction methodology has been established for them. Specifically, applying prior work to LLM inference latency prediction on NPUs faces three challenges: undisclosed microarchitecture of commercial NPUs, unpredictable compiler optimizations, and latency non-linearity induced by bucketing.
  We present LENS, a latency estimator that predicts NPU inference latency without information on the microarchitecture or compiler, and captures the non-linear latency induced by bucketing. LENS profiles each bucket with two end-to-end (E2E) measurements and composes the results to predict latency for arbitrary input-output length combinations. We validate LENS across NPUs from multiple vendors, several LLMs, and diverse workloads, achieving a mean prediction error of 2.15\%. We further compare LENS against two methodologically related baselines, confirming the validity of its approach.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Beyond Parallel Sampling: Diverse Query Initialization for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17209)
*Sidhaarth Murali,João Coelho,Jingjie Ning,João Magalhães,Bruno Martins,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DivInit方法，通过在第一轮生成多样化查询来缓解标准并行采样中的查询冗余问题，在5个开源模型和8个基准测试中，相比标准并行采样，在多跳问答任务上平均提升5-7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准并行采样在智能体搜索的广度扩展中存在收益递减问题，原因是第一轮生成的查询高度冗余，导致检索到的证据重叠，后续轮次基于共享信息进行推理，限制了多样性和性能提升。

Method: DivInit是一种无需训练的第一轮干预方法：从单次调用中生成n个候选查询，从中选出k个多样化种子（k < n），作为并行轨迹独立运行。

Result: 在5个开源权重模型和8个基准测试上，DivInit在匹配计算量的条件下，多跳问答任务平均提升5-7个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过简单且无需训练的多样化第一轮查询初始化，可以有效解决并行采样中的收益递减问题，提升智能体搜索的广度扩展效果。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for agentic search typically increases depth (i.e., more turns and tokens per trajectory) or breadth (i.e., more parallel rollouts). Here we focus on breadth scaling, showing that standard parallel sampling yields diminishing returns, tracing this to query redundancy at the first turn. When models issue similar first queries across rollouts, the threads retrieve overlapping evidence, and subsequent turns are conditioned on this shared retrieval. We address this limitation with DivInit, a training-free intervention at the first turn. Rather than sampling k independent first queries, DivInit draws n candidates from a single call, picks k < n diverse seeds, and runs them as parallel trajectories. Across five open-weight models and eight benchmarks, DivInit consistently improves over standard parallel sampling, with average gains of five to seven points on multi-hop QA at matched compute. Code available at https://github.com/cxcscmu/diverse-query-initialization

</details>


### [111] [When Rules Learn: A Self-Evolving Agent for Legal Case Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17220)
*Mingxu Tao,Jiawei Hu,Xian Zhou,Wenpeng Hu,Jiajun Cheng,Yunbo Cao,Zhunchen Luo,Guotong Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需参数训练的自演化框架，通过LLM代理自动生成并优化查询重写规则，显著提升BM25在法律案例检索中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管密集检索模型取得进展，但BM25在法律检索领域仍是强基线；现有查询重写方法依赖人工规则或贪婪选择，缺乏系统性自动优化机制。

Method: 基于LLM代理的自演化框架：配备自动评估环境，迭代执行规则创建、组合验证实验、基于历史反馈消除无效规则，全程无需参数训练。

Result: 在中文法律案例检索基准LeCaRD-v2上，该方法优于非演化基线（包括人工设计规则和贪婪规则选择），且高性能核心LLM下优势更显著。

Conclusion: LLM利用历史实验结果的能力及其内生的规则消除知识，是自演化过程中优化规则集的关键机制。

Abstract: Legal case retrieval remains challenging due to the complexity of legal language and the need for precise lexical alignment between queries and relevant cases. Although dense retrieval models have achieved notable progress, empirical studies show that BM25 continues to serve as a strong baseline in this domain. It motivates us to propose a self-evolving framework for rule-driven query rewriting that enhances BM25 without any parameter training. The framework equips an LLM-based agent with an automatic evaluation environment, enabling it to iteratively create rewriting rules, plan validation experiments over rule combinations, and eliminate ineffective rules based on historical feedbacks. We evaluate our method on the Chinese legal case retrieval benchmark LeCaRD-v2. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms non-evolutionary baselines, including human-designed rules and greedy rule selection, particularly when powered by a highcapacity core LLM. We also conduct detailed analyses to investigate the mechanisms underlying self-evolution. Our findings reveal that LLM's capabilities to leverage previous experimental results and its intrinsic knowledge of rule elimination play critical roles in refining the rule set via self-evolution.

</details>
